# Skills · R · part 12

919 entries, realize-aggregate to recall-journal. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **realize-aggregate**: BLOCK-TYPE skill of the mismAgent worker (build, matrix §13.A). Realizes an Aggregate root + value objects — the block that OWNS the rule: invariants live HERE, written and tested once (invariant-test, one per invariant). State with private setters, invariant_fields confined (named predicate exposed, not the raw field), no deletion (soft-delete). Primitive/Published Language identity at the boundary, value class internal only. Carries the §14 gate (invariant-bearing state captive of the root). Loaded by the worker when block.type = aggregate. ([lucolucus/mismagent](https://github.com/lucolucus/mismagent/tree/HEAD/plugins/mismagent/skills/realize-aggregate))
- **realize-application-service**: BLOCK-TYPE skill of the mismAgent worker (build, matrix §13.A). Realizes a thin command/use-case (Application Service) — does NOT re-implement an aggregate's rule: it goes through the root that owns it (previous block) and reads the other context ONLY via the port's interface. AC-test with a fake port. 'Green on its own' includes that the Aggregate's invariant-tests stay green. Loaded by the worker when block.type = application-service. ([lucolucus/mismagent](https://github.com/lucolucus/mismagent/tree/HEAD/plugins/mismagent/skills/realize-application-service))
- **realize-port**: BLOCK-TYPE skill of the mismAgent worker (build, matrix §13.A). Realizes a Port — consumer-owned interface, read-only, in PRIMITIVES (never the supplier's types) — the declaration of the inter-context boundary. Carries the spec of the consumer-driven contract test: reusable abstract class + factory, with a fake for the green-on-its-own. The projection (§13.B) decides whether the port stays in-process or is projected into OpenAPI. Loaded by the worker when block.type = port. ([lucolucus/mismagent](https://github.com/lucolucus/mismagent/tree/HEAD/plugins/mismagent/skills/realize-port))
- **realize-read-model**: BLOCK-TYPE skill of the mismAgent worker (build, matrix §13.A). Realizes a read-model — query/projection (CQRS) whose output respects the view_shape pinned in the manifest. - **realize-scaffold**: BLOCK-TYPE skill of the mismAgent worker (build, matrix §13.A). Loaded by the worker when block.type = scaffold. ([lucolucus/mismagent](https://github.com/lucolucus/mismagent/tree/HEAD/plugins/mismagent/skills/realize-scaffold))

Use when a task needs step-by-step analysis, troubleshooting, diagnosis, or understanding of causes, risks, and tradeoffs (for turning analysis into a recommendation or plan deliverable, see planning-patterns); e.g. Stepwise Decomposition, Chain-of-Thought Request, Assumption Listing, Root Cause Analysis, Hypothesis Generation, Decision Tree, Tradeoff Analysis, or Failure Mode prompts. ([aeron100/prompt-patterns](https://github.com/aeron100/prompt-patterns/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-patterns))
- **reasoning-patterns-v2**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-llm/reasoning-patterns-v2) · ★729)
- **reasoning-patterns-v2-agentgptsmith-monadframework-3**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-llm/reasoning-patterns-v2-agentgptsmith-monadframework-3) · ★729)
- **reasoning-personas**: Activate different high-agency thinking modes to unlock better reasoning. Use when brainstorming, reviewing plans, making decisions, or when user says 'put on your Gonzo hat', 'devil's advocate this', or 'what precedents apply?' ([sundial-org/awesome-openclaw-skills](https://github.com/sundial-org/awesome-openclaw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-personas) · ★662)
- **reasoning-plan-then-execute**: Use before tackling any multi-step, ambiguous, or high-stakes task — decompose it into an explicit, ordered plan of small sub-steps and solve them one at a time (checking each before moving on) instead of jumping straight to a final answer. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-plan-then-execute) · ★1)
- **reasoning-planning**: Thinking, planning, and decision-making patterns extracted from Codex Plan Mode, Claude Code, and Gemini CLI. ([subhansh-dev/agent-maxxing](https://github.com/subhansh-dev/agent-maxxing/tree/HEAD/system-prompts/03-reasoning-planning) · ★2)
- **reasoning-reflexion**: Use after producing any draft with a right/wrong dimension (code, math, structured extraction, factual analysis) and before it ships — runs a dedicated critique pass that hunts for errors in the draft, then a separate correction pass that fixes only what the critique found. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-reflexion) · ★1)
- **reasoning-router**: Classify problems and route to optimal thinking MCP (11 available). Monitor confidence, re-route on stall. Use for complex reasoning. NOT for simple questions (answer directly) or code review (review). ([wyattowalsh/agents](https://github.com/wyattowalsh/agents/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-router) · ★5)
- **reasoning-rubric-grader**: Use before shipping any deliverable that has checkable success criteria (code diffs, data extraction, structured reports, answers with hard constraints like word limits or required citations) — define an explicit scoring rubric, grade the draft against it with evidence, and only deliver once it clears a stated bar; otherwise revise and re-grade (bounded rounds). Skip for purely subjective/creative tasks with no checkable criteria. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-rubric-grader) · ★1)
- **reasoning-sandwich**: Template for pre-reasoning → action → post-verification model allocation ([baekenough/oh-my-customcode](https://github.com/baekenough/oh-my-customcode/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reasoning-sandwich) · ★42 · 2 copies)
- **reasoning-scratchpad-cot**: Use for any task with a checkable right answer that involves arithmetic, multi-step logic, multi-hop lookups, or a plan with dependent steps — think step by step in a separate scratchpad before writing the final answer, instead of jumping straight to a conclusion. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-scratchpad-cot) · ★1)
- **reasoning-self-consistency**: Use when a task has one objectively checkable correct answer (math, logic, classification, extraction, multiple-choice, deterministic code/calculation output) and a single-pass answer is unreliable — generate several independent reasoning attempts and keep the majority answer instead of trusting the first one. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-self-consistency) · ★1)
- **reasoning-semiformally**: Apply semi-formal certificate reasoning to code analysis — patch verification, fault localization, patch equivalence. Use when reviewing patches, hunting bugs across scopes, comparing fixes, or when code reasoning requires tracing execution across files/modules. Triggers on code review, bug localization, patch comparison, name shadowing, scope analysis, regression checking. ([oaustegard/claude-skills](https://github.com/oaustegard/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/code-intelligence/skills/reasoning-semiformally) · ★149 · 2 copies)
- **reasoning-semiformally**: Apply semi-formal certificate reasoning to code analysis — patch verification, fault localization, patch equivalence. Use when reviewing patches, hunting bugs across scopes, comparing fixes, tracing execution across files/modules, analyzing name shadowing or scope resolution, or checking for regressions. ([antstanley/skills](https://github.com/antstanley/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/reasoning-semiformally/skills/reasoning-semiformally) · 2 copies)
- **reasoning-step-back**: Use when a question is gated by an underlying general principle, law, rule, doctrine, or framework (physics, law, strategy, medicine, economics) that must be correctly identified before the specific case can be solved — especially when jumping straight to the specific numbers/facts risks skipping or silently misapplying that principle. Not for pure lookup, formatting, or extraction tasks with no governing concept. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-step-back) · ★1)
- **reasoning-strategy-selection**: Select and configure the right reasoning strategy, native FC behavior, and output quality pipeline for any task type. ([tylerjrbuell/reactive-agents-ts](https://github.com/tylerjrbuell/reactive-agents-ts/tree/HEAD/apps/docs/skills/reasoning-strategy-selection) · ★28)
- **reasoning-surface**: Author the episteme gate artifact (.episteme/reasoning-surface.json) correctly on FIRST contact — schema, lens order, honest values, strict-vs-advisory, and when to escalate to epistemic-interrogation instead. Use the moment a PreToolUse hook reports a missing or stale Reasoning Surface, or BEFORE starting a session's first high-impact operation. Triggers on "reasoning surface", "Episteme Strict Mode", "surface stale", "blocked by episteme", "게이트 막힘", "서피스 갱신". ([junjslee/episteme](https://github.com/junjslee/episteme/tree/HEAD/skills/custom/reasoning-surface) · ★17)
- **reasoning-swarm**: Use when a decision or proposal has genuine multi-stakeholder stakes, spans multiple teams, or carries organization-scale or hard-to-reverse consequences — build-vs-buy calls, architecture decisions affecting several teams, policy with systemic impact, or when the user explicitly wants independent parallel takes stress-tested against each other rather than one recursive pass. Skip for single-claim analysis or solo stress-testing (use reasoning-framework instead), time-pressured decisions, mechanical tasks, or when a recommendation is wanted rather than a tour of considerations. ([ravenoak/llm-skills](https://github.com/ravenoak/llm-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/reasoning-swarm/skills/reasoning-swarm))
- **reasoning-templates**: When delegating a sub-task to an agent or drafting a prompt and the user wants a proven structure instead of an ad-hoc one — "write me a prompt for this", "structure this request", "which prompt pattern should I use", spawning a sub-agent, or setting up a role for research, tool-driven work, or expert output. Provides four battle-tested patterns with fill-in placeholders: CO-STAR for structured expert deliverables, ReAct for tool-using step reasoning, expert-persona + chain-of-thought for domain judgment, and zero-shot CoT as the universal fallback. ([201924611/agent-core-skills](https://github.com/201924611/agent-core-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-templates))
- **reasoning-tools-kb**: A comprehensive library of 100+ transferable reasoning primitives extracted from 34 high-value domains. Covers: Decision Under Uncertainty, Persuasion & Influence, Creative Generation, Complex Systems, Skill Mastery, Coordination & Cooperation, Truth-Seeking & Verification, Conflict & Competition, Resource Allocation, and Pattern Recognition. Each tool includes what it is, why it matters, the key mental move, classic and surprising applications, and failure modes. Use when facing any complex reasoning challenge, decision under uncertainty, or when you need to apply structured thinking framewo… ([onfire7777/universal-ai-skills-library](https://github.com/onfire7777/universal-ai-skills-library/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-tools-kb) · ★16)
- **reasoning-trace**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/business/reasoning-trace) · ★729)
- **reasoning-trace-optimizer**: Debug and optimize AI agents by analyzing reasoning traces. Activates on 'debug agent', 'optimize prompt', 'analyze reasoning', 'why did the agent fail', 'improve agent performance', or when diagnosing agent failures and context degradation. ([Kushal9889/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Kushal9889/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/context-engineering/examples/interleaved-thinking) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **reasoning-trace-optimizer**: Debug and optimize AI agents by analyzing reasoning traces, context degradation, tool confusion, instruction drift, repeated task failures, and performance regressions. ([muratcankoylan/Agent-Skills-for-Context-Engineering](https://github.com/muratcankoylan/Agent-Skills-for-Context-Engineering/tree/HEAD/examples/interleaved-thinking) · 3 copies)
- **reasoning-trace-recorder**: Auto-invokes for every Claude turn during active T4 task execution. Emits append-only trace events (file_read, output_claim, tool_call, decision) to .claude/plans/traces/{TASK_ID}.jsonl. Source of grounding-discipline checks. Disable: AF_TRACE_DISABLED=true. ([nguyenthienthanh/aura-frog](https://github.com/nguyenthienthanh/aura-frog/tree/HEAD/aura-frog/skills/reasoning-trace-recorder) · ★24)
- **reasoning-tree-of-thoughts**: Use when a problem has several genuinely different candidate approaches and an early wrong turn is expensive or hard to undo — planning, puzzles, architecture/strategy trade-offs, combinatorial decisions, scheduling, multi-criteria choices. Searches a tree of partial solutions, scores each branch with a cheap evaluator, prunes the weak ones, expands only survivors, and can backtrack. Do NOT use for single-path problems, factual lookup, or when you just want several independent full attempts voted at the end (that is self-consistency, a different skill in this pack). ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-tree-of-thoughts) · ★1)
- **reasoning-verifier**: Verify LLM reasoning using Reverse Chain-of-Thought (RCoT) to detect overlooked conditions. ([majesticlabs-dev/majestic-marketplace](https://github.com/majesticlabs-dev/majestic-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/majestic-tools/skills/reasoning-verifier) · ★50)
- **reasoning-verifier**: Check completed analyses for omitted requirements, unsupported assumptions, and faulty conclusions. ([majesticlabs-dev/majestic-abilities](https://github.com/majesticlabs-dev/majestic-abilities/tree/HEAD/plugins/reasoning/skills/reasoning-verifier) · ★1)
- **reasoning-verifier-loop**: Use when a draft answer must be trusted before it ships — especially from a cheap/weak model, on math/logic/factual claims, or whenever being wrong is costlier than being slow — by having a genuinely skeptical verifier try to refute the draft and forcing a targeted retry on real failures until the answer survives or a retry budget runs out. ([firststone-vc/reasoning-pack](https://github.com/firststone-vc/reasoning-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning-verifier-loop) · ★1)
- **reasoning-with-parallel-attack-paths**: Use when orchestrators need diverse security analysis by running the SAME attacker prompt across MULTIPLE LLMs (Claude, GPT-4, Gemini, DeepSeek) in parallel using LiteLLM ([LeoDPraetorian/praetorian-coverage-digest](https://github.com/LeoDPraetorian/praetorian-coverage-digest/tree/HEAD/.claude/skill-library/agentic-attack-workflows/reasoning-with-parallel-attack-paths) · ★1)

Use when building self-learning agents, optimizing workflows, or implementing meta-cognitive systems.
